# BR JARVIS — Comprehensive File-by-File Forensic Audit

This document contains the forensic audit record for all files and directories in the BR JARVIS repository as mandated by Phase 0 of the Master Modernization Directive.

**Total Repository Files Audited:** 1987  
**Total Python Source Files:** 512  
**Total Lines of Code / Config:** 2039529  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Top 20 Highest-Impact Findings

1. **Multiple Historical Entrypoints & Architectural Splintering**: `main.py`, `main_mk37.py`, `start.py`, `server.py`, `ui.py`, `ui_mark.py`, `float_widget.py`, `brjarvis.py` lead to disjoint startup lifecycles.
2. **Duplicated AI Backend & Routing Subsystems**: `backends/` (raw provider classes) exists alongside `gateway/` (model gateway) and `router/` (smart router) with circular cross-references.
3. **Actions vs Tools Layer Duplication**: 58 legacy action files in `actions/` and 63 tool files in `tools/` connected via `tools/legacy_actions_tools.py` adapter.
4. **Memory Subsystem Fragmentation & Data Leakage**: 28 files in `memory/` with uncoordinated stores plus personal data files (`contacts.json`, `contacts.enc`) committed in tree.
5. **Unsafe Hardcoded Credentials & Machine Paths**: Hardcoded API keys in `config/api_keys.json`, `config/models.json`, `connectors/github.py` and local machine paths (`C:\Users\bhara`).
6. **Broken Guardian Hash Invariant**: Guardian integrity check fails in test suite due to desynchronized `.guardian_hashes.json` against modified files.
7. **Sandbox-to-Host File Boundary Failures**: Risk of sending sandbox-internal paths to host browsers (`ERR_FILE_NOT_FOUND`) without strict export verification.
8. **Circular Import Dependencies**: 15 circular import chains detected across actions, tools, router, plugins, and UI modules.
9. **Dead & Unreferenced Modules**: 93 unreferenced internal python modules and obsolete legacy files (`main_mk37.py`, `requirements_mk37.txt`).
10. **Lack of Strict Postcondition Verification**: Vague execution booleans where model completion was conflated with true environmental goal achievement.
11. **Voice Pipeline Fragmentation & Stale Audio**: Disjoint voice modules (`gemini_live`, `gemini_stt`, `whisper_local`, `silero_vad`, `tts`, `tts_queue`) lacking unified barge-in queue flushing.
12. **Vision Hierarchy Inefficiency**: Screen capture indiscriminately sent to cloud VLM without prior perceptual caching or local OCR/DOM verification.
13. **Root Pollution**: 52 files in repository root including obsolete spreadsheets, business research documents, and redundant scripts.
14. **Lack of Model-Agnostic Capability Routing**: Hardcoded provider assumptions and capability inference from model names.
15. **Unstructured Provider Error Handling**: Generic failures obscuring root cause HTTP status, rate limit, quota, and authentication diagnostics.
16. **Task Decomposition Gap**: Complex compound commands executed without multi-stage DAG task tracking and checkpoint recovery.
17. **Permissions & Policy Decoupling**: Security logic split between `permissions.py` at root and `security/policy_engine.py`.
18. **Unsafe Shell Execution Risks**: Unsanitized subprocess commands susceptible to argument injection and path traversal.
19. **Memory DB Artifacts in Git**: Stale pickled objects, databases, and binary caches committed in `memory_db/`.
20. **Documentation Inconsistencies**: Contradictory architectural claims (e.g. '100% local' vs cloud backends, outdated test statistics).
